review_id: 2200
entity_slug: ice-house-taproom
google_review_name: places/ChIJt5yVynYFmogRVmNUYl8vQM4/reviews/ChdDSUhNMG9nS0VJWG9oWV9lN2NqempBRRAB
rating: 5
review_text: Absolutely! Here’s a concise, engaging Google review based on your feedback:

---

We’ve visited Ice House Taproom three times and always had a great experience. The view of the sunset over the bay is beautiful, and we even spotted an alligator one evening! The food is consistently good—especially the pork wings, chicken wings, burgers, and Mexican salad. Prices are reasonable and not touristy, and the atmosphere feels local and welcoming. We enjoyed some live music during dinner, and the owner/manager Anthony always greeted us warmly. My only suggestion is for the bartenders to be a bit more attentive, as we often had to go to the bar ourselves. Overall, a fantastic spot off the beaten path with great food and views. We’ll definitely be back!

Sources

review_text: Went last night during happy hour, and had what started out as a good time. The food was pretty good, starting with the Santa Fe rolls, then a burger and street tacos. All the food was actually quite good, and both the burger and street tacos were on the happy hour menu. (Relevant later.)
The beers were pretty expensive at $9 each, but oh well.
Where it all went south, and the reason for the 1 star, is that Monica, our server, charged us regular price for the two items on that were on both the HH menu and regular menu. Why, we asked? Her response is the reason I needed to post: "Because you didn't ask for the happy hour price."

Super shady, check your receipt. Better yet, don't go.

reviewer_name: Ivan Hernandez
rating: 5
body: Absolutely! Here’s a concise, engaging Google review based on your feedback:

---

We’ve visited Ice House Taproom three times and always had a great experience. The view of the sunset over the bay is beautiful, and we even spotted an alligator one evening! The food is consistently good—especially the pork wings, chicken wings, burgers, and Mexican salad. Prices are reasonable and not touristy, and the atmosphere feels local and welcoming. We enjoyed some live music during dinner, and the owner/manager Anthony always greeted us warmly. My only suggestion is for the bartenders to be a bit more attentive, as we often had to go to the bar ourselves. Overall, a fantastic spot off the beaten path with great food and views. We’ll definitely be back!
